| 0x0800023C | `sub_0800023C` | InitializeRootTaskAndEnterScheduler | confirmed | game | asm/nonmatchings/sched/sub_0800023C.s: Initializes the root task as the scheduler-list head, clears current-task state and sets the task count to one. This is not an ordinary returning initialization helper. The saved callback r0 slot receives the current-task-global address left in r1, and the r1 slot receives incoming r2; no generalized root-argument ABI is inferred.; tools/config/functions.cfg: Original _08000268/_0800026C/_08000270 identify list head 0x03001240, task count 0x03001244 and current-task pointer 0x0300123C. Canonical boundaries and counts are unchanged; no runtime or equivalent C claim.; src/main.c: Matched AgbMain initializes the root control callback to sub_08001B78, allocates its stack, stores stack pointer base +0x3FC and invokes this scheduler initializer. |
| 0x08000274 | `sub_08000274` | StartTask | confirmed | game | asm/nonmatchings/sched/sub_08000274.s: Returns without restarting a task already present in the scheduler list. Otherwise coerces a zero scheduling-order key at task +8 to one, marks task +4 ready, inserts the record using the +8 order key and increments the task count. Builds an initial 0x30-byte saved context with incoming r1/r2 becoming callback r0/r1 and sub_0800035C as the return address. Other initial saved register slots are not initialized here. The dispatcher later restores the context and enters callback +0xC; this API does not immediately execute it.; tools/config/functions.cfg: Original _08000296 compares existing/new +8 fields by subtraction and BPL to choose insertion position. _080002BE increments count and constructs context. This configured function range also contains the shared dispatcher _080002E0.._08000342, reached from the root initializer and task-switch paths; it is not executed by every StartTask call. Static original-ASM and caller evidence only. Canonical boundaries and counts are unchanged; no runtime or equivalent C claim. |

| 0x08005A24 | `sub_08005A24` | TaskDimPaletteBanksUntilReleased | confirmed | game | src/code_08002118.c: Treats low/high halfwords of range as first palette bank and bank count. Saves count*32 bytes from the palette shadow, sets hold word 0300001C to1 and starts factor 1024. The target is the low halfword of floor((low 16(percent)*1024)/100). While factor exceeds target, scales saved RGB5 channels into the shadow, preserving bit 15, reduces the factor toward target and submits palettes with one scheduler wait per iteration. The last written factor need not equal target because the step is applied after drawing. If target>=1024 this scaling loop is skipped. Then waits for the hold word to become zero; re-reads its bit 1 before conditionally restoring saved colors, submits palettes, waits once and frees the backup. The hold is not checked during the dimming loop. No bank, allocation, percent-range or fixed-time guarantee.; tools/config/functions.cfg: 0x08005A34..48 unpack banks and compute/truncate target; 5A50 sets hold; 5A54/5A6C allocate/copy backup; 5A76 tests factor, 5A9A..5AEC write RGB, 5AF8..5B20 step/wait. The mode argument controls flag bit 1; its full meaning is not asserted.; tools/config/functions.cfg: 0x0800BD62 captures the task count; 0x0800BDBA..0x0800BDDA initialize scene subsystems. 0x0800BDF8..0x0800BE0E test the three exit conditions. 0x0800BE42 sets the stop request before pool cleanup. 0x0800BEA8..0x0800BEAE wait on the original count. Scheduler waits do not promise exact elapsed frames.
